# Changelog

All notable changes to the Petra open rules dataset are recorded here.
Entries are generated automatically by the publisher
(`.github/workflows/open-rules-publish.yml`) from a field-level JSON diff
between this dataset's previous and current published state — per-corridor,
per-field (added/removed/changed requirement or manual-checklist item, tier
change, review-date change). No hand-written entries.

<!-- entries appended below by the publisher; newest first -->

### united-states-to-northern-ireland

- Review date changed: 2026-06-24 → 2026-09-22.

April 1992 über das Halten von Hunden (Hundegesetz; HG) — Art. 9, Art. 10","retrieved_at":"2026-09-03","applies_to":"dogs kept in Liechtenstein (the LI leg of the Amicus registration duty)","supporting_requirements":["Art. 10(1)-(2): every dog microchipped and, within 10 days, reported by the vet to and registered in the database designated by the Amt für Lebensmittelkontrolle und Veterinärwesen","Art. 10(6): the details of identification and registration otherwise follow SWISS animal-disease legislation (i.e. the TSV above, including its holiday/short-stay exemption)","Art. 9: dogs over three months are registered with the commune of RESIDENCE"]},{"url":"https://www.mauren.li/files/attachments/anmeldung-hund-homepage-20230110.pdf","authority":"Gemeinde Mauren (Liechtenstein) — executing commune under HG Art. 2/Art. 9","page_title":"Info zur Anmeldung und Haltung von Hunden","retrieved_at":"2026-09-03","applies_to":"identifying WHICH database the Liechtenstein ALKVW has designated under HG Art. 10(2)","supporting_requirements":["\"Jeder Hund in Liechtenstein muss bei der AMICUS-Hundedatenbank angemeldet werden.\"","the Halter-ID needed to register is issued by the Gemeindekasse (i.e. presupposes a commune of residence)","registration is performed by a vet in FL or Switzerland"]}].

- Tier label changed: Guidance only → Source-checked · 2026-09-19.

### united-states-to-new-zealand

- Manual checklist item removed: "RNATT sample 3–24 months before shipment".
- Tier changed: guidance → qualified.
- Review date changed: 2026-09-05 → 2026-09-19.

In case that the animal has a valid booster vaccination, It does not require an interval of 21 days...\" / \"The test result should not be less than (0.5 IU / ml).\" / \"The validity of the Rabies Testing certificate is 365 days...\"","\"the minimum age for importing from the high risk countries is not less than 15 weeks\" / \"The vaccination should be done at age not less than 12 weeks\" (new fact, not gated)","\"received preventive doses for internal and external parasites during the 14 days prior to shipment...\"","\"Personal import is allowed for no more than 2 cats or 2 dogs or a dog and a cat annually...\""]}].
